# Full topics: compact domain → level → DAG

36,628 topics, exact names/levels/order retained. Each base-36 short ID is its 1-based position, not a graph ID. Level is inherited from the enclosing header. See meta_rules_and_index_sorted_compact.md; noise IDs there remain reconciliation-only.

Level legend: H=HS; U=UG; J=UG+PG; P=PG-only; I=industry; ?=level-unknown (not reclassified).
